# Qt vs React/Tauri parity matrix (MIB Studio)

Baseline: origin/develop `5f906ac2` (2026-11-04, PR #834). Worktree `/home/gavin/Developer/.worktrees/parity-20261104`, read-only.

> **Snapshot.** This describes develop `5f906ac2` (2026-11-04). File:line references apply to that commit (#871 has since split `desktop/src/App.tsx`), and the open Tauri parity PRs of program #869 (#870, #872–#898) may close gaps listed here.

Qt side: `src/frontend/**`, `include/frontend/**`, `resources/ui/*.ui`. Tauri side: `desktop/src/**`, `desktop/src-tauri/src/**`, `crates/mib-app-commands`, `crates/mib-bridge*`.
Machine-readable copy: `parity-matrix.json` (same rows, extra keys `id`, `area`, `dup_of`); suspicious Tauri code: `suspicious-tauri-code.json`. Both are on P5 under `/mnt/hdd/developer-data/mib-studio-qt/parity-20261104/inventory/`, not in the repo.

## How this was produced, and its limits

- Static source reading only. Nothing was built or run, so every status is what the code says, not what an operator saw. No hardware, no GUI.
- Seven parallel readers each took one area, read the Qt `.cpp`/`.h`/`.ui` in full, and searched the Tauri code for each behaviour; the merger spot-checked rows (pump polling, FCS, Monitoring Settings menu stub, Latest-Frame acknowledgement, wafer grid, px->um on exports, 6 random PRESENT rows) and re-verified that every cited `file:line` exists (11 out-of-range line ends were clamped).
- Status: PRESENT = same behaviour; PARTIAL = exists but differs (the note says how); MISSING = no equivalent; TAURI-ONLY = new in Tauri (not a Qt gap).
- `severity_if_missing` is the impact if the feature were absent, and is given on every row (also PRESENT/TAURI-ONLY rows): blocker = an operator cannot do a job they can do in Qt; major = workable but materially worse or less safe; minor = convenience.
- Some features appear in several areas (for example the recording-capacity panel). Duplicates are kept in the file but marked `dup_of`, and are excluded from the 'unique' counts.
- Tauri has two review surfaces (the in-App Review tab and the separate YOFO Review app) and a remote mode (browser against a PZ7035 board). Rows say which surface implements a feature.
- Rows whose note says UNVERIFIED rest on an inference the reader could not confirm: SHELL-008, SHELL-021, SHELL-076, SHELL-097, CONNECT-033, CONNECT-040, CONNECT-049, CONFIG-033, CONFIG-049, CONFIG-053, CONFIG-063, CONFIG-077, CONFIG-089, PREVIEW-010, PREVIEW-015, PREVIEW-017, PREVIEW-026, PREVIEW-058, MON-012, MON-070, REVIEW-017, REVIEW-063, HW-004, HW-023, HW-045, HW-046.

## Headline findings

The prior docs (`react-readiness-2026-09-23.md`: 'software parity is now substantially complete'; `2026-09-23-tauri-replacement.md`: overlays, buffer save and monitoring marked done) are not supported by the code on develop. The Tauri UI covers the main capture, start, finalize, review and export path, but a large share of Qt's operator-facing behaviour is absent or reduced, and the Qt features merged since 2026-10-01 are mostly not ported (the last desktop change on develop is 2026-10-22).

Only one gap meets the strict 'operator cannot do a job they can do in Qt' bar: **Export FCS and Batch Export FCS (#576)** (REVIEW-043, REVIEW-044). The Tauri bridge exposes `all|images|charts|metrics_csv` only, although the shared `HdfExportService` already supports FCS. Whether FCS is a routine job is Gavin's call.

Themes with the most major-severity gaps:

1. **Live tuning on the Monitoring tab.** Qt has a tune panel that applies area/deformability/ring/target-gate/Young's-modulus criteria live while a run is on screen, a scatter with gate overlay and draggable gate edges (#831), click-to-view (#705), 25 valid and invalid cell thumbnails with rejection reasons, zoom/pan, KDE and a Monitoring Settings dialog. Tauri's Monitoring sub-tab has charts but no tune panel (it links to Preview > configuration, which is locked during a run), no gate overlay, no click-to-view, no cell strips, and the Settings > Monitoring Settings menu item only switches tab (App.tsx:1440). See MON-044, MON-052..053, MON-064..066, MON-069..082.
2. **Acquisition-mode safety.** The backend refuses Start in Latest Frame mode unless the caller sets `acknowledgeLatestFrameDrops`; Qt asks (Switch / Continue / Cancel) and shows a permanent EVERY FRAME / LATEST FRAME badge; Tauri never sets the flag (grep finds it only in `src/frontend` and the coordinator), has no badge, and the delivery mode can only be changed by editing raw config JSON. SHELL-054, SHELL-055, CONNECT-019.
3. **Recording-capacity panel and gates (#833/#834)**: window, fps, save stream, buffer frames/size/seconds, destination write rate and four Start gates. Not in Tauri (gates appear only as an inline readiness line). CONNECT-053, MON-028.
4. **Overview camera window.** No drag-ROI for eGrabber, no script write-back, profile `camera.window` does not drive the rectangle (#833), no Overview camera-script editor, no camera-script auto-apply on Overview/Experiment tab switch, and no Wafer Grid / dot-grid localisation (#472; the backend reports `dot_grid (Qt shell only)`). CONNECT-048..058, CONFIG-044, CONNECT-054.
5. **Hardware controls.** Pump and nanopositioner controls exist only on the Connect tab (Qt: a sidebar on every tab), need Service mode plus a one-shot arm for Run/Purge/Enable autofocus/Jog, are locked during an experiment (Qt: always live), and the pump panel never polls the device, so run/stall state goes stale after Run (HardwareControls.tsx:70-95, 154; Qt polls every 500 ms at SyringePumpTab.cpp:276-288). Pump settings, Modbus address scan UI, Ctrl+Shift+H, Boot Service Toggles and the XGC+R5D live-view preset are missing. HW-007..018 (HW-015 is the polling gap), HW-040.
6. **Review.** Tauri has two partial surfaces: the in-App tab (7 metric columns vs Qt's 24, SVG density scatter, no thumbnails, no click-to-view) and YOFO Review (thumbnails, click-to-view, 25 selectable columns, but no regenerate preview canvas, and its batch export writes no charts). The in-App single-file exports send the *live* px->um factor (App.tsx:2370, 2375-2377; backend uses it as-is at HdfExportService.cpp:330,406), whereas Qt uses the file's recorded factor (HdfReviewTab.cpp:1296,1341,1844), so um^2 columns and charts are wrong when the file was recorded at a different factor (REVIEW-068).
7. **Config and profiles.** Save writes only `image_processing` of an opened file, not the whole `config.json` (CONFIG-014); a profile's camera script is saved but never loaded or applied; no `config.json` file watcher (CONFIG-094); no core-line selector (Contract 1 vs 2, #492; CONFIG-082); invalid-frame sampling and MindVision exposure/fps quick fields missing.
8. **Preview/playback.** Paused and scrubbed frames are drawn raw with no overlay (PREVIEW-022; the docs' 'overlays done' is overstated); the ROI cannot be drawn by dragging (numeric X/Y/W/H only) and is not persisted to `config.json`; no 'Rec: N saved, M skipped' counters; Record/Save Buffer gating differs.
9. **Shell.** No Diagnostics dialog (the telemetry is not exposed), no global alert banner, no single-instance guard, no Open Logs Folder, no window-geometry persistence, no in-app installer download or version pinning, backend-init failure goes only to the log drawer.

TAURI-ONLY areas worth knowing (not gaps): Service/Commissioning mode with one-shot arming, guided workflow stepper with hardware preflight and context bar, remote mode against a PZ7035 (token gate, viewer-only banner, take control, SSD runs, ring playback, run quick look), central methods (registry draft/review), Young's-modulus histogram, YOFO Review thumbnails/density colours/review-inspect contract.

## Prior-doc claims that the code contradicts

- `docs/exec-plans/active/2026-09-23-tauri-replacement.md:19` marks 'Preview pause/scrub, buffer save, overlays and background calibration' done. Paused/scrubbed frames carry no overlay (PREVIEW-022), the ROI cannot be dragged, and Save Buffer needs capture stopped (PREVIEW-036).
- `docs/architecture/react-readiness-2026-09-23.md` ('software parity substantially complete'; 'Camera script/MindVision JSON editing, pump/autofocus controls ... are now wired'): wired, but gated to Service mode, Connect tab only, and the pump panel is not polled.
- `docs/exec-plans/active/2026-10-08-playback-save-parity.md`: its Qt-side and Tauri-side claims about playback hold (no play/pause, no step buttons, raw paused frames, `display_fps` default 30 vs 60); its line numbers are stale.
- `desktop/src/components/ReviewCharts.tsx:38` still says the charts are the same as Qt; `App.tsx:1567` still says the nanopositioner panel 'lands with UI-3 (#268)' although `HardwareControls` exists.

## Suspicious or stubbed Tauri code

103 entries from the area readers (full list with reasons in `suspicious-tauri-code.json`; a repo-wide grep for TODO/FIXME/not implemented found no markers, so these are behavioural). Highest-impact first:

- `desktop/src/components/HardwareControls.tsx:70-95`: Pump status is read from a cached backend value every 1 s but the device is only polled by the manual 'Read device status' button; `start()`/`purge()` do not set `run_status`, so the panel keeps saying Stopped after Run.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:2370, 2375-2377`: In-App Review exports pass the live `pixel_to_micron` as `conversion_factor`; the backend uses it verbatim, so um^2 values are wrong for files recorded at another factor (Qt: file's recorded factor).
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:1440`: 'Monitoring Settings...' menu item only switches tab; there is no dialog.
- `desktop/src/components/hardwareControlModel.ts:16-23`: Service-mode and experiment gates for pump/autofocus exist only in the webview; the backend handlers check neither.
- `desktop/src/components/HardwareControls.tsx:180-181,200`: Stop pump, Stop purge and Disable autofocus are disabled while another hardware command is in flight (the stage Stop is not), delaying a safety stop.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:1229, 1237`: `experimentCompleted` constant false and `cameraExpected` hard-coded to '' in the workflow/context-bar facts.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:1802-1811`: Every preflight recovery button just calls `refreshPreflight`.
- `desktop/src/preflight.ts:360-363`: Storage preflight check is a permanent stub on desktop.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:642-650`: Discovery `valid`/`complete` are computed (discovery.ts) but never read, so an incomplete scan looks like a full one.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:1368`: `setServiceMode(...).catch(() => {})` swallows a failure to enter Service mode in the backend.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:2047, 2009`: Overlay legend does not match drawn colours; 'Save clip' is permanently disabled ('not available yet').
- `desktop/src/profiles.tsx:64`: Dead 'restore' branch.
- `desktop/src/review/ReviewPanel.tsx:241-245`: A failed open leaves stale `info` from the previous file.
- `crates/mib-app-commands/src/lib.rs:1111, desktop/src-tauri/src/lib.rs:467,472,907-908`: `fetch_background`/`background_bytes` still registered and return `FRAME_PROTOCOL_UPGRADE_REQUIRED`; `bridge.fetchBackground` is never called, so the background image is never shown.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:2227, 2239`: `||1` / `||1000` silent fallbacks without range checks (periodic trigger test).
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:1342, 1464`: Operator name is empty; 'Report a Problem' opens a different repository from Qt.

By area (all entries):

**Shell: menus, tabs, status bar, updates, help, startup** (22)

- `desktop/src/App.tsx:1229` [hardcoded] const experimentCompleted = false: the Experiment stage can never become 'complete' after a finished run, so workflow.ts:226,234 'complete' branches are dead and the 'Workflow complete' bar is effectively unreachable.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:1342` [stub] operatorName: '' ('operator identity not captured yet'): the Operator segment of the context bar (contextBar.ts:166-177) is permanently 'pending'.
- `desktop/src/contextBar.ts:97` [stub] Fallback text 'Experiment profiles arrive with UX-2 (#306)' is shown when no profile is active, although profiles are implemented (profiles.tsx); stale placeholder wording.
- `desktop/src/contextBar.ts:202` [stub] Idle Storage segment is always 'pending' with 'free-space check pending a backend contract', even though instrument.storage free_bytes is already polled (App.tsx:1259-1263).
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:1237` [stub] cameraExpected: '' hard-coded ('profile-declared expected identity - UX-2 follow-up'); the preflight camera-identity comparison cannot run.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:1306` [hardcoded] FOCUS_STALE_MS = 1000 is a conservative default because ring_ratio_stale_ms 'is not polled into the shell yet'; quality-gate staleness ignores the configured value.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:1567` [other] Sidebar section tooltip still says 'Nanopositioner control panel lands with UI-3 (#268)' although controls exist in the Connect tab (HardwareControls/StageControls); stale placeholder text.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:1368` [swallowed-error] void bridge.setServiceMode(...).catch(() => {}): if the backend refuses, the UI still shows Service mode while raw LED values stay refused; mismatch is invisible to the operator.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:1056` [swallowed-error] fetchPlatformInfo().catch(() => setPlatform(null)) falls back to DESKTOP_CAPABILITIES (platformCapabilities.ts:11-24): a transient failure on a PZ7035 would show host-only surfaces (background, autofocus, realtime switch).
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:555-558` [swallowed-error] setPreferences(...).catch(() => {}) and getPreferences().catch(() => {}) at 565-573 hide a write failure or a malformed preferences.json (platform.rs:56-66 returns an error) - persisted shell prefs are silently dropped.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:461-462` [other] BackendError events (including a raw-recording 'Recording save failed' from BackendFacade.cpp:331-335) are only appended to the 50-line log drawer; no alert, no latched fault (Qt raises save.fatal and blocks the next Start).
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:349-354` [other] Log is capped at 50 entries and mirrored to desktop-shell.log per line; tick() appends 'tick error' every 500 ms on a persistent failure (App.tsx:505), evicting useful lines within ~25 s and spamming the file.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:1438` [dead-code] 'Processing Settings...' (1438), 'Pixel to Micron...' (1439) and 'Updates...' (1447) share one handler that only sets tab/expTab/configTab; three menu entries land on the same unfocused panel.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:1464` [hardcoded] Report a Problem URL points to github.com/gavinlouuu-kpt/mib-studio-qt, while Qt (MainWindow.cpp:359) and mkdocs.yml use KPT1020/mib-studio-qt; one of them is probably wrong for operators (UNVERIFIED which).
- `desktop/src/closeGuard.ts:41` [other] Comment says 'Warn before leaving mid-run' but the beforeunload handler (line 44) warns only for busy/dirty UI state, not for an active experiment; closing the last browser tab stops and saves the run on the server without a prompt.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:533` [swallowed-error] bridge.monitoringSetActive(...).catch(() => {}): a failed visibility toggle leaves accumulation on/off out of step with the Monitoring tab, with no message.
- `desktop/src/OfflineHelp.tsx:28` [other] Custom regex Markdown renderer has no nested lists/italics; docs/release-notes/v1.2.0.md (lines 14-27) uses indented sub-bullets that render as a single run-on item in What's New.
- `desktop/src/OfflineHelp.tsx:5` [other] Bundles docs/manual/*.md as the Tauri User Manual; those pages describe Qt-only UI (Ctrl+Shift+H, Start Live View, Boot Service Toggles, Open Logs Folder, window memory - getting-started.md lines 36-48,56-66) that Tauri lacks.
- `desktop/src-tauri/src/app_update.rs:107` [other] Update check fetches https://updates.yofo.bio/<channel>/tauri/<os>-<arch>/latest.json; docs/exec-plans/active/2026-09-23-tauri-replacement.md says no Tauri feed was published. Operators may only ever see a fetch error (UNVERIFIED current feed state).
- `desktop/src/coreManagement.tsx:20` [hardcoded] Update/registry channel is useState('stable') and registry URL defaults to https://updates.yofo.bio; neither is persisted or derived from the installed build (Qt derives the default from the version, AutoUpdater.cpp:93-100).
- `desktop/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json:22` [other] 'csp': null disables the Content-Security-Policy for the webview (also no window minWidth/minHeight at lines 14-19).
- `desktop/src-tauri/src/lib.rs:767` [other] tauri::Builder has no on_window_event / RunEvent::Exit hook and no single-instance plugin; backend shutdown depends on the BackendBridge destructor running at process exit (UNVERIFIED).

**Connect and Overview tabs, camera control** (14)

- `desktop/src/App.tsx:703` [swallowed-error] onConnectPicked returns silently when the picked key is no longer in the discovery list (e.g. after a rescan): Connect does nothing and shows nothing.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:642-650` [swallowed-error] refreshCameraState never reads CameraDiscovery.valid/complete (discovery.ts:98-104): a cancelled or incomplete scan renders like an empty one; a refused scan (busy while capturing) only reaches the collapsed log.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:1747` [other] Configure Mock button is disabled only on !ready: it can swap the camera source while capture runs or an experiment is active; the backend ConfigureMockCamera (BackendFacade.cpp:708) has no check either (Qt refuses).
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:1741` [other] Connect disabled while running/experiment/script-busy with no tooltip explaining why (title is only set for 'Pick a device first').
- `crates/mib-app-commands/src/lib.rs:1417` [other] select_hardware_camera has no capture-running / experiment guard while select_mindvision_camera, apply_camera_script and reset_hardware_camera do (lib.rs:1431, 1451, 1616); only the JS button disables it.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:811` [swallowed-error] start failed / select failed / configure failed / stop capture failed are only append()ed to the log drawer, which is collapsed by default (showLog=false); Qt raises a modal 'Camera' warning.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:1096` [swallowed-error] refreshCameraGeometry catches every error and returns null; the Overview polls it every 2 s (App.tsx:1167) so a failing geometry call silently leaves the window box/mode line stale.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:1802-1811` [other] Every preflight recovery button (kind 'refresh' or 'retry') just calls refreshPreflight; for 'No camera selected' the Refresh does not rescan devices (refreshCameraState).
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:1237` [stub] cameraExpected is hard-coded '' ('profile-declared expected identity - UX-2 follow-up'), so the preflight camera identity warning branch (preflight.ts:136) can never trigger.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:1229` [dead-code] experimentCompleted is the constant false, so the workflow 'Complete' state for the Experiment stage is unreachable.
- `desktop/src/preflight.ts:360-363` [stub] Storage check is 'not-required: pending a backend status contract' whenever instrument.storage is absent (always on the desktop build), so storage is never actually checked there.
- `desktop/src/cameraScript.tsx:19-20` [other] Disabled-reason text sends MindVision users to 'the JSON editor below', but the Overview tab renders only CameraScriptControls; the JSON editor is in Experiment > Config.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:1306` [hardcoded] FOCUS_STALE_MS = 1000 is hard-coded (comment: autofocus ring_ratio_stale_ms not polled), so the Overview focus gate ignores the configured staleness.
- `desktop/src/components/StartupDiscoveryControls.tsx:6` [hardcoded] Auto-select default is false (localStorage only); Qt always runs startup selection. The checkbox exists only when caps.autofocus (HardwareControls.tsx:115), so there is no opt-in on instruments without autofocus.

**Configuration, profiles, processing settings, cores** (10)

- `desktop/src/profiles.tsx:64` [dead-code] 'operation==="restore"?"restore":"list"' can never pick "restore": the restore operation returns at line 62 before this line.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:623-627` [swallowed-error] onApplyRoi coerces blank or non-numeric X/Y/W/H to 0 via 'Number(x) || 0'; a blank width/height becomes 0, which is the 'clear ROI' command, with no warning.
- `desktop/src/cameraScript.tsx:56` [other] Reset Camera (GenICam DeviceReset) runs with no confirmation dialog; Qt asks 'Proceed?' (ConfigTabs.cpp:1035-1046). Only the stopped-camera guard protects the operator.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:1946` [other] Second CameraScriptControls (path-based Apply, no saved-revision check) duplicates the checked document editor Apply at App.tsx:2168; two different 'Apply script to camera' paths with different safeguards.
- `desktop/src/bridge.ts:1036-1040` [dead-code] calibrationRecordsList/Get/Apply are wired to the bridge but no component calls them (only calibrationRecords.ts:149 helper); the calibration-record apply flow has no operator UI.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:1438-1447` [other] Settings menu 'Processing Settings…', 'Pixel to Micron…' and 'Updates…' all just open the same Configuration > app tab (setConfigTab('app')); there is no menu entry for Processing Core.
- `desktop/src/cameraDocument.tsx:37` [swallowed-error] Save As swallows every error from reading the target path (permission, I/O) as 'file does not exist' and then tries 'create'; relies on native create to reject collisions.
- `desktop/src/components/PulseGeneratorControls.tsx:19-20` [swallowed-error] Status poll errors are dropped (setStatus(null)) so a failed bridge call shows only 'Status unavailable' with no error text or log entry.
- `src/backend/app/ProcessingCoreManagement.cpp:174` [hardcoded] Activation checks plugin contract_version against the compile-time MIB_PROCESSING_CONTRACT_VERSION (also line 204), so the Tauri path can never activate a Contract 2 core that Qt offers via the core-line selector.
- `src/backend/app/ConfigDocumentApply.cpp:302-303` [other] Apply report lists display_fps as 'not applied (Qt display setting)' although the UI reads display_fps from the active profile (App.tsx:1202) for the preview FPS limit; the report is misleading. dot_grid really has no Tauri effect.

**Experiment > Preview, playback, ROI, background, buffer** (9)

- `desktop/src/App.tsx:2047` [dead-code] Target/Valid/Invalid legend swatches (#2b6cb0/#1a7f37/#b42318) are shown for host processing, but ProcessedPreview draws cyan contours, a pink target box and a green-tinted mask (ProcessedPreview.tsx:17-24); the legend matches nothing drawn.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:2009` [disabled] RingPlaybackPanel is rendered without ssdPathAvailable, so 'Save clip' is disabled forever with a static reason 'Saving needs the SSD (not available yet)' (ringPlayback.ts:343-347, RingPlaybackPanel.tsx:173). PZ7035 only.
- `crates/mib-app-commands/src/preview_buffer.rs:9` [other] save_preview_buffer holds the global bridge mutex for the entire TIFF/AVI write (even under spawn_blocking in lib.rs:752); fetch_preview_buffer and other bridge calls queue behind it, so UI polling can stall during a long save. Impact UNVERIFIED.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:455` [other] applyEvents ignores FrameReady(BackgroundCapture) and RecordingStatus events (eventAdapter.ts:76,82 decode them); background_set is only re-read by refreshConfig, so 'No background set' / disabled Clear Background go stale after auto capture and no recording counters exist.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:846` [swallowed-error] Record, Set/Clear Background, Clear ROI, Apply ROI failures only append() a line to the Log drawer, which is collapsed by default (App.tsx:181, 2580); the operator sees no dialog or inline error. Qt uses QMessageBox for the Record errors.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:1052` [other] expActive covers Starting/Active/Stopping only, so ROI/background controls look enabled in the Failed (unacknowledged) state while the backend refuses; Qt withIdleConfiguration also locks Failed and says 'acknowledge its fault'.
- `crates/mib-app-commands/src/lib.rs:1111` [stub] fetch_background and background_bytes always return Err('FRAME_PROTOCOL_UPGRADE_REQUIRED') yet stay registered in dispatch.rs:121-122,670-671. bridge.fetchBackground (bridge.ts:943) uses fetch_background_packet but no UI calls it, so the background image is never displayed.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:259` [hardcoded] previewFpsLimit defaults to 30 and follows only profiles.activeProfile.display_fps (App.tsx:1202); Qt default is 60 (resources/defaults/config.json:81) and live-config edits retune it (AppConfigWatcher.cpp:625-630).
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:760` [swallowed-error] PZ run-preview poll swallows every fetchRunPreview error ('a capture can time out'); a persistent failure leaves only the 'Waiting for the PL cell capture...' hint with no error text.

**Experiment start/stop and Monitoring tab** (17)

- `desktop/src/App.tsx:1440` [stub] Settings > 'Monitoring Settings...' only switches to Experiment > Monitoring sub-tab; no dialog, no MonitoringSettings bridge call exists (Qt dialog edits chart/refresh/thumbnail settings).
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:2303-2309` [stub] 'Processing settings' group is a button to another tab plus a 'locked during an experiment' note; the Qt tune panel (criteria, Apply/Revert, live apply) has no counterpart on the monitoring tab.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:533` [swallowed-error] bridge.monitoringSetActive(...).catch(() => {}) hides a failure to switch the backend monitoring producer on/off; operator sees an empty chart with no reason.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:542-544` [swallowed-error] Snapshot/trigger poll catch is empty; comment says 'next tick will surface it' but nothing ever surfaces a persistent failure (stale charts look like an idle run).
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:1031` [swallowed-error] fetchRunAccounting('last_run').catch(() => null): accounting failure silently falls back to status-only outcome text, so lost-frame/denominator detail disappears without a notice.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:539` [hardcoded] fetchMonitoringSnapshot(200): rolling window fixed at 200 rows (Qt keeps 1000 and exposes ranges in Monitoring Settings); also hardcoded as LIMIT in MonitoringCharts.tsx:6.
- `desktop/src/components/MonitoringCharts.tsx:21-24` [hardcoded] Ratio and Young's-modulus histograms use a fixed 10 bins; Qt bin count is configurable in Monitoring Settings.
- `desktop/src/components/MonitoringCharts.tsx:51-53` [dead-code] Invalid-object cross branch (r.valid false) is unreachable: data.scatter is built from valid rows only (line 9), so invalid objects are never plotted.
- `desktop/src/components/MonitoringCharts.tsx:25` [dead-code] 'invalid' field (bounded.length - scatter.length) is returned but never read; the footer shows only 'uncalibrated' (line 55). Invalid count is not shown anywhere.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:1342` [hardcoded] operatorName: '' with comment 'operator identity not captured yet'; Start/identity metadata (#712/#728) cannot carry an operator name.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:1567` [other] Stale tooltip: 'Nanopositioner control panel lands with UI-3 (#268)' on a section that is already live behind caps.autofocus; misleading wording.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:2227` [hardcoded] Number(pulseUs) || 1: a blank or invalid pulse width silently becomes 1 us and is sent with no range check or message (Qt spin box enforces min/max).
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:2239` [hardcoded] Number(periodicMs) || 1000: a blank or invalid period silently becomes 1000 ms and is sent with no range check or message.
- `desktop/src/experimentStart.ts:32` [other] Readiness refusal message lists only Fail/Unavailable gates; Warn gates are dropped, so advisory readiness warnings Qt explains (explainReadiness) never reach the operator.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:2234-2287` [other] Periodic-trigger test is not stopped when leaving the Monitoring sub-tab or when the experiment ends (UNVERIFIED whether the backend auto-stops); Qt stops it on tab hide (ExperimentMonitoringTab.cpp:1253-1272).
- `desktop/src/bridge.ts:1019-1031` [dead-code] fetchMonitoringCells, monitoringCellPin/Unpin/ClearPins and fetchMonitoringCellImage have no UI consumer (only monitoringCells.ts helpers); the backend cell strip and pins are not rendered anywhere.
- `desktop/src/bridge.ts:813` [dead-code] experimentCancel bridge function has no caller in desktop/src (only a comment in eventAdapter.ts:96); no Cancel control exists.

**Review tab, exports, regenerate masks** (15)

- `desktop/src/review/ReviewPanel.tsx:241-245` [other] Failed open: ReviewSession::open() already closed the previous file (ReviewSession.cpp:514), but the panel keeps the old `info` (reviewing stays true) and ReviewApp shows the new failed path (onFileChange at :239). Only a log line reports it. UNVERIFIED at runtime.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:1045-1046` [swallowed-error] fetchRunAccounting('review').catch(() => null): a read failure silently drops the run-outcome notice (#367/#584 safeguard).
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:103` [hardcoded] H5_FILTER = [{extensions:['h5']}] is used by Select HDF File (:969); .hdf5 recordings are hidden (YOFO and the batch picker use h5+hdf5).
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:2370` [other] Single-file exports pass stats.pixel_to_micron from the LIVE processing stats (also :2376-2377); when stats are invalid the factor is omitted and the backend's live default is used, never the file's recorded factor.
- `desktop/src/exportControls.tsx:59` [hardcoded] keep_partial_on_failure:true is hard-coded for every export, no operator choice.
- `desktop/src/exportControls.tsx:54` [hardcoded] Metrics save dialog defaultPath is the literal 'metrics.csv' (not <source>_metrics.csv, no collision-free suggestion).
- `desktop/src/components/ReviewCharts.tsx:38` [other] Text claims the calibration is the 'current backend setting, same as Qt', but Qt uses the file's recorded factor since TD-17; backend src/backend/app/ReviewCharts.cpp:42 feeds the live factor.
- `desktop/src/review/ReviewApp.tsx:38` [hardcoded] DEFAULT_PX_TO_UM = 0.4886 hard-coded fallback factor; Qt falls back to the host's live processing factor.
- `desktop/src/review/charts/ChartsView.tsx:17` [hardcoded] DEFAULT_CORE_FRACTION 0.9 (and density args (1.0, 0.9, 8, true) at :85) hard-coded; Qt reads QSettings Monitoring/KdeCoreFraction (HdfReviewTab.cpp:2437).
- `desktop/src/review/charts/chartData.ts:14-23` [swallowed-error] loadCurves() turns an isoelastic-curve fetch failure into [] with no message; curves vanish from the view and from Export All / Export Charts snapshots silently.
- `desktop/src/review/FrameViewer.tsx:58-59` [swallowed-error] seriesCount(...).catch(() => setSeriesCount(0)) hides the series navigation on error without any log line.
- `desktop/src/review/exports/ExportDialogs.tsx:91` [swallowed-error] 'Show in folder' ignores a failed revealItemInDir (.catch(() => {})); the button just does nothing.
- `desktop/src/reanalysisControls.tsx:15` [hardcoded] Default input budget 4096 frames / 256 MiB: a whole-file regenerate of a larger file fails until the operator raises it (Qt has no cap).
- `src/backend/review/ReviewJobs.cpp:951-957` [other] YOFO batch Export All builds `base` without generateReviewCharts / supplementalImages, so no scatter/histogram TIFFs are written per file (HdfExportService.cpp:311-316); Qt writes both.
- `desktop/src/components/SavedReviewImage.tsx:6` [other] ROI overlay checkbox defaults to ON (useState(true)); Qt's Show ROI defaults off, and the facade draws it yellow 1 px (ReviewCharts.cpp:17).

**Syringe pump, nanopositioner/autofocus, pulse generator** (16)

- `desktop/src/components/HardwareControls.tsx:70-95` [other] Pump status is only the backend cache (fetch_pump_status); nothing polls the device on a timer. Only the manual 'Read device status' button (:154) calls pump_poll_status. SyringePumpService::start/purge do not set run_status (cpp:375-406; only pollStatus does, 614-623), so after Run the panel still says Stopped, `stopped` (:125) stays true, Start/Purge/rate stay enabled and stalls are missed. Qt polls every 500 ms.
- `desktop/src/components/HardwareControls.tsx:180-181` [other] 'Stop pump', 'Stop purge' (and 'Disable autofocus' :200) are disabled={busy||!ready}; busy covers any in-flight command (run() :98-109, HardwareCommandOwner), so Stop is greyed while a slow Connect/Apply is pending although the intro text promises Stop stays available. StageControls.stop() (SC:94-107) avoids this; pumps/autofocus do not.
- `desktop/src/components/hardwareControlModel.ts:16-23` [other] Service-mode / experiment-lock / arming rules for pumps, autofocus and pulse generator exist only in the webview. handlePumpCommand/handleAutofocusCommand (BackendFacade.cpp:2101-2405) check neither mode nor experiment state; only pulse generator uses withIdleConfiguration (:4141). A direct bridge call (browser/remote) bypasses the lock. UNVERIFIED for the remote server path.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:1567` [stale] Sidebar title says 'Nanopositioner control panel lands with UI-3 (#268)', but the control panel already exists (HardwareControls.tsx:185-208). Stale tooltip.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:1304-1313` [hardcoded] FOCUS_STALE_MS = 1000 with comment 'ring_ratio_stale_ms is not polled into the shell yet'; HardwareControls already fetches the config (HC:88) and AutofocusConfig has ring_ratio_stale_ms. Alignment quality gate ignores the configured staleness.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:1368` [swallowed-error] bridge.setServiceMode(...).catch(() => {}) swallows a refusal; the UI shows Service/Operator regardless, and a later LED apply fails with a less direct message.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:2206` [other] Event log prints 'manual sort pulse fired (${pulseUs} us)' using the typed field, but the backend duration only changes via the separate 'Set Pulse' button (:2224-2233). Log can state a duration that was never applied.
- `desktop/src/App.tsx:2227` [hardcoded] Number(pulseUs) || 1 and (:2239) Number(periodicMs) || 1000 silently replace blank/0/NaN input; no range checks (Qt: 1-1,000,000 us and 10-60000 ms); backend only enforces >=1.
- `desktop/src/components/HardwareControls.tsx:16` [hardcoded] initialConnection() gives address '1' to both pump slots (reused at :41,:49); Qt defaults Sheath to 2 (SyringePumpSettingsDialog.ui:155). Two pumps on one RS485 bus collide until edited.
- `desktop/src/components/HardwareControls.tsx:197` [hardcoded] OEABT connect passes literals (-1, 115200, 1) for com port, baud and address instead of the fields.
- `desktop/src/components/HardwareControls.tsx:18` [hardcoded] DEFAULT_MICROLITERS_PER_REV '25' is sent as microlitersPerRev even for syringe pumps (:150) where it is meaningless; comment says it stands until a measured calibration replaces it.
- `desktop/src/components/HardwareControls.tsx:163` [other] `status?.microliters_per_rev ?? Number(calibrations[id])` does not fall back when the status reports 0, so rpm entry throws. Also units[id] stays 200 (rpm) after switching the slot to Syringe (select has no rpm option at :111) and 'Apply rate' still treats the number as rpm.
- `desktop/src/components/HardwareControls.tsx:207` [other] 'persistence across restarts is not provided by this command': autofocus parameters (incl. focus setpoint, persisted by Qt NanopositionerTab.cpp:387-392) are lost at restart unless also written in the config document.
- `desktop/src/components/PulseGeneratorControls.tsx:42` [other] Discovery request sets addressFrom=addressTo=typed address, so the pulse-generator 'Find endpoints' can never scan a range (Qt scans 1-16, ConfigTabs.cpp:1702-1703) or reveal a mis-addressed device.
- `desktop/src/components/PulseGeneratorControls.tsx:48` [hardcoded] Channel list [0,1,2,3] and frequency range 400-40000 (:51) duplicate backend constants (PulseGeneratorService.h:39-41, BackendFacade.cpp:4125-4126).
- `desktop/src/components/StartupDiscoveryControls.tsx:6` [other] Auto-select hardware on startup defaults to false (localStorage); no hardware is auto-connected on a fresh profile, unlike Qt (MainWindow.cpp:726). Behavioural default, not a bug.
